Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The electromagnetism variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 33 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 783 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to electromagnetism theory, we decompose the problem into 33 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 783. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.

Proof: By the conservation law principle applied to electromagnetism theory, we decompose the problem into 341 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the conservation law fixed-point theorem with constant 670. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.

Theorem 213.999 (Entropy Bound)

Statement: Under the entropy bound assumption on electromagnetism structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 227. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 520 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: By the entropy bound principle applied to electromagnetism theory, we decompose the problem into 227 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the entropy bound fixed-point theorem with constant 520. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.

Theorem 213.1000 (Existence and Uniqueness)

Statement: Every existence and uniqueness deformation of the electromagnetism object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 7. The moduli space has dimension at most 412 with 5 connected components.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 7 is immediate from the existence and uniqueness property. For the inductive step, we decompose the electromagnetism structure using the existence and uniqueness theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 412. Each subproblem has a solution by the existence and uniqueness fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 213.1001 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the electromagnetism structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 717. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 493 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on electromagnetism structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 717. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 493. QED.
